```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

```java
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```java
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
```

```java
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure

            return -1;

        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```java
public class TitleCase {
    public static char[] titleCase(char[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return null;

        int n = arr.length;
        char[] ret = new char[n];

        boolean wordStart = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            char c = arr[i];
            if (Character.isWhitespace(c)) {
                ret[i] = c;
                wordStart = true;
            } else if (wordStart) {
                ret[i] = Character.toTitleCase(c);
                wordStart = false;
            } else {
                ret[i] = Character.toLowerCase(c);
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```

```java
        if (arr == null) return null;
```

```java
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; Character.isSpaceChar(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; !Character.isSpaceChar(arr[i]) ==> (\result[i] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[i]) || \result[i] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[i])));
```
```java
    //@ ensures \fresh(\result);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    public static char[] titleCase(char[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return null;

        int n = arr.length;
        char[] ret = new char[n];

        boolean wordStart = true;
```
```java
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> ret[j] == arr[j]);
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; !Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> (ret[j] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[j]) || ret[j] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[j])));
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            char c = arr[i];
            if (Character.isWhitespace(c)) {
                ret[i] = c;
                wordStart = true;
            } else if (wordStart) {
                ret[i] = Character.toTitleCase(c);
                wordStart = false;
            } else {
                ret[i] = Character.toLowerCase(c);
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

    //@ ensures \result != null && \result.length == arr.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; Character.isWhitespace(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; !Character.isWhitespace(arr[i]) ==> (\result[i] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[i]) || \result[i] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[i])));
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; Character.isUpperCase(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);  
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i <= arr.length; Character.isLowerCase(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[i]));  
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 < i <= arr.length; Character.isTitleCase(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[i]));  
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; Character.isDigit(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length; Character.isLetter(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[i]) || \result[i] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[i]));  
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; !Character.isLetter(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; !Character.isISOControl(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; Character.isISOControl(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; Character.isSpaceChar(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; !Character.isSpaceChar(arr[i]) ==> (\result[i] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[i]) || \result[i] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[i])));
    //@ ensures \fresh(\result);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    public static char[] titleCase(char[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return null;

        int n = arr.length;
        char[] ret = new char[n];

        boolean wordStart = true;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> ret[j] == arr[j]);
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; !Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> (ret[j] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[j]) || ret[j] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[j])));
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            char c = arr[i];
            if (Character.isWhitespace(c)) {
                ret[i] = c;
                wordStart = true;
            } else if (wordStart) {
                ret[i] = Character.toTitleCase(c);
                wordStart = false;
            } else {
                ret[i] = Character.toLowerCase(c);
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> ret[j] == arr[j]);
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; !Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> (ret[j] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[j]) || ret[j] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[j])));

    //@ ensures \result != null && \result.length == arr.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; Character.isWhitespace(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; !Character.isWhitespace(arr[i]) ==> (\result[i] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[i]) || \result[i] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[i])));
 
 
 
 
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; Character.isLetter(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[i]) || \result[i] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[i]));  
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; !Character.isLetter(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);  
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i < arr.length; !Character.isISOControl(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);  
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i <= arr.length; Character.isISOControl(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);  
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 - 1 <= i < arr.length; Character.isSpaceChar(arr[i]) ==> \result[i] == arr[i]);  
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length; !Character.isSpaceChar(arr[i]) ==> (\result[i] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[i]) || \result[i] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[i])));  
    //@ ensures \fresh(\result);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    public static char[] titleCase(char[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return null;

        int n = arr.length;
        char[] ret = new char[n];

        boolean wordStart = true;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> ret[j] == arr[j]);
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; !Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> (ret[j] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[j]) || ret[j] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[j])));
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            char c = arr[i];
            if (Character.isWhitespace(c)) {
                ret[i] = c;
                wordStart = true;
            } else if (wordStart) {
                ret[i] = Character.toTitleCase(c);
                wordStart = false;
            } else {
                ret[i] = Character.toLowerCase(c);
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

    public static char[] titleCase(char[] arr) {

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> ret[j] == arr[j]);
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i; !Character.isWhitespace(arr[j]) ==> (ret[j] == Character.toTitleCase(arr[j]) || ret[j] == Character.toLowerCase(arr[j])));
